Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the mass_spec structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 799. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 666 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 799 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the mass_spec structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 666. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
